# Skills · S · part 69

803 entries, sector-and-stock-screening to secure-craft. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sector-and-stock-screening**: Map an industry or theme and screen for stock ideas with value, growth, quality and red-flag criteria, value-chain mapping and crowding checks. Use for 行业分析 or 选股, not for one company's results. ([Kukutx/superskills](https://github.com/Kukutx/superskills/tree/HEAD/plugins/finance/skills/sector-and-stock-screening))

- **sector-brief**: Produce a Sector Brief HTML report for any industry sector using Moody's GenAI MCP tools and web research. Use this skill whenever the user asks to analyze a sector, write a sector report, do an industry analysis, create a sector overview, or generate a sector deep-dive. Trigger even if they just name a sector and mention "analysis", "overview", "outlook", "report", or "deep-dive". Also trigger for phrases like "what's happening in the retail sector" or "give me a sector breakdown for aerospace". ([MA-Auth-External/moodys-skills](https://github.com/MA-Auth-External/moodys-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sector-brief) · ★1)

- **sector-linkage-analysis**: 板块间联动关系实证分析法（状态依赖型虹吸）。回答「A 板块起来，B/C 板块会怎样」这类板块传导、资金分流、跷跷板、风格轮动问题。核心是把问题从「A 涨 B 怎样」升级为「A 涨时，取决于大盘水位，B 有两种截然相反命运」，并用反证检验否定双向跷跷板、用剥离 beta 的超额相关判定因子重叠。触发词：板块联动、板块关系、跷跷板、虹吸、资金分流、风格轮动、A起来B会怎样、板块相关性、同因子板块。 ([lyonliqima/caisen-skill](https://github.com/lyonliqima/caisen-skill/tree/HEAD/sector-linkage-analysis) · ★8)
